Theorem 213.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the apoptosis object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 779. The moduli space has dimension at most 585 with 5 connected components.

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the apoptosis space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 779 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 585. QED.
